A non-zero exit code will only be issued if validation could be completed successfully and the file was invalid.\n73: \n74: * v8r always exits with code `99` when:\n75:     * The input file was validated against a schema and the input file was **invalid**\n76: \n77: ## FAQ\n78: \n79: ### ❓ How does `v8r` decide what schema to validate against if I don't supply one?\n80: \n81: 💡 `v8r` queries the [Schema Store catalog](https://www.schemastore.org/) to try and find a suitable schema based on the name of the input file.\n82: \n83: ### ❓ My file is valid, but it doesn't validate against one of the suggested schemas.\n84: \n85: 💡 `v8r` is a fairly thin layer of glue between [Schema Store](https://www.schemastore.org/) (where the schemas come from) and [ajv](https://www.npmjs.com/package/ajv) (the validation engine). It is likely that this kind of problem is either an issue with the schema or validation engine.\n86: \n87: * Schema store issue tracker: https://github.com/SchemaStore/schemastore/issues\n88: * Ajv issue tracker: https://github.com/ajv-validator/ajv/issues\n89: \n90: ### ❓ What JSON schema versions are supported?\n91: \n92: 💡 `v8r` works with JSON schema draft-04, draft-06 and draft-07.\n93: \n94: ### ❓ Will 100% of the schemas on schemastore.org work with this tool?\n95: \n96: 💡 No. There are some with [known issues](https://github.com/chris48s/v8r/issues/18)\n97: \n98: ### ❓ Can `v8r` validate against a local schema?\n99: \n100: 💡 Nope.
